Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The networks operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 337. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 883 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the networks space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 337 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 883. QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 388. QED.
